ALWAYS use this skill when creating, editing, syncing, reviewing or repairing Arete cards, when running any `arete` command, or when asked to show a card in Obsidian. - **areview**: Launch parallel review agents over the work done so far in this session and synthesize their findings. N adversarial agents each try to break the work from a different inferred perspective (data-loss, races, failure-paths, ungrounded claims); a standing quality agent always runs alongside them surfacing reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups. The two come back in separate buckets so cleanups never crowd out real defects. Use when the user wants a multi-angle review of the current session's changes (code or design/docs) before committing/shipping — e.g. "/areview 4". The number sets how many… ([Stvad/knowledge-medium](https://github.com/Stvad/knowledge-medium/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/areview) · ★1)
